- **solo-brief**: Turns a rough founder ask into something an agent can actually run. Use when a request is vague, contains several asks at once, has no success condition, or is about to be handed to an agent or automation: 'here's what I want to do', 'can you help me with this', a long paragraph of mixed problems, or before dispatching any multi-step task. Also use when the founder asks to grade, sharpen, or rewrite a prompt they wrote. Do NOT use for a crisp single-step request that is already clear, and when a dedicated agent-prompt grader such as prompt-grader is installed, hand the harness-level rubric to… ([Donsoleil/soloagency](https://github.com/Donsoleil/soloagency/tree/HEAD/skills/solo-brief))

Repo-specific strategy in .claude/rules/branch.md takes precedence. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-branch-commit) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-code-review-process**: Reference: standard code review pipeline (CodeRabbit + multi-agent review + the user's review perspectives) and the per-finding decision rules. Severity and confidence scores are inputs to a per-item decision, NOT auto-skip filters. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-code-review-process) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-design-criteria**: Reference: criteria for validating feature design across scope clarity (Goal and AC alignment), dependency identification, and technical feasibility against the project stack. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-design-criteria) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-dod**: Reference: soloscrum Definition of Done checklist. AC verified at the appropriate layer (Subtask PR slice + no regression; Issue-without-Subtasks full AC; parent Issue intent-level sign-off when all Subtasks close). Tests exist if applicable, PR body links Issue number with Closes/Fixes (per-Subtask PRs reference only the Subtask, never the parent), lint passes, review approved. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-dod) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-issue-format**: Reference: Issue and Subtask format. An Issue records intent (Background / Goal / Acceptance Criteria / Out of Scope); a Subtask slices work (Parent / What / Checklist / Notes). Includes the Issue-vs-Subtask discriminator and the two AC shapes (user-facing / structural). ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-issue-format) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-issue-size**: Reference: Issue and Subtask split criteria. Issues split when they bundle multiple independent intents (SP &gt; 5 or subtask count &gt; 5 are mis-scope smells, not hard work-volume limits). /soloscrum:breakdown fires when delivering one intent as a single PR would be unreviewable. Defines the suggest_split action, including the source Issue's terminal disposition after a split. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-issue-size) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-pr-lifecycle**: Reference: PR phases (draft → review → ready → merge-handoff) and per-transition autonomy rules. Reversible transitions (gh pr create --draft, gh pr ready) run without pre-confirm; irreversible transitions (gh pr merge) are gated to the user. Used by implement-task, review-implementation, and the code-review-process verdict mapping. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-pr-lifecycle) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-priority**: Reference: priority levels Urgent/High/Medium/Low and decision criteria. Urgent=blocker or outage, High=user impact or dependency, Medium=normal dev, Low=tech debt. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-priority) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-review-perspective**: Reference: the Review Perspective format — a machine-local, self-describing unit of review knowledge stored at ~/.claude/review-perspectives/&lt;name&gt;/PERSPECTIVE.md. Defines the directory contract, the skill-shaped frontmatter, the description rules /soloscrum:review selects on (English, &lt;=2048 chars, when + what + a stated boundary, decidable without the body), and why perspectives are deliberately not stored as skills. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-review-perspective) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-define-story-points**: Reference: story point scale and estimation criteria. SP is anchored on scope x uncertainty (size class, not a time unit); SP 5 is the upper bound and Issues estimated above it are mis-scope smells signalling likely intent bundling — return to /soloscrum:refine for Issue split per soloscrum-define-issue-size. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/tree/HEAD/skills/soloscrum-define-story-points) · ★1)
